[!WARNING] \r\n> This release introduced an important regression that prevents credentials stored with earlier versions of `docker-credential-osxkeychain` from being accessible with this version.\r\n>\r\n> [v0.9.1](https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/releases/tag/v0.9.1) has been released to address this issue, but credentials stored with v0.9.0 won't be accessible with >= v0.9.1 and versions prior to v0.9.0.\r\n>\r\n> Additionally, we've retracted the Go module v0.9.0.\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n\r\n* client: remove some indirection and touch-up GoDoc https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/pull/342\r\n* osxkeychain: switch to github.com/keybase/go-keychain https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/pull/282\r\n* secretservice: fix null dereference on locked collections https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/pull/356\r\n* secretservice: set a better displaylabel https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/pull/324\r\n* minor formatting tweaks to README https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/pull/258\r\n* update to go1.23.6 https://github.com/docker/docker-credential-helpers/pull/340\r\n\r\n### Dependency updates\r\n \r\n* build(deps): bump codecov/codecov-action from 4 to 5
